## Post 141063 by Zerebral — 2025-07-24T06:15:00Z
<!-- url: https://forum.turtlecraft.gg/viewtopic.php?p=141063#p141063 | page 11 | era: pre-1.18.1 -->

The biggest problem is the "no server reset" theme. it doesn't match with new players joining BGs to face Naxx geared players with 6500 hp and 90 dps weapons.

You should be able to join and be on par with everyone else. How are you going to do Rated PvP when you face players who have been playing for 6+ years with twice the stats that you have. There's STILL no skill involved and it's going to be a complete joke.

The ONLY true way to scale this is like Guild Wars 2 does: Create a BG lounge. You're level 60 and get to choose gear With FIXED max to stats in here.
Different sets with a different stats should be added for experimentation and MAX the stats to compare with tier 1 as it's the most balanced since people do tournaments with this.

But add some items with fun variations like:
<https://database.turtlecraft.gg/?item=13505>
<https://database.turtlecraft.gg/?item=65008>
<https://database.turtlecraft.gg/?item=13361>

This way you DON'T need to create a resilience like system and you instantly fix gearing imbalance. From here you have a MUCH easier time to balance out classes.

## Post 141087 by 987004590 (Barrens Chat Casualty) — 2025-07-24T11:00:00Z
<!-- url: https://forum.turtlecraft.gg/viewtopic.php?p=141087#p141087 | page 11 | era: pre-1.18.1 -->

Xfaction is absolutely the shittiest idea.

A warrior only fights with all their might for the cause they belong to when they feel a sense of belonging. Therefore, any battlefield must have two different groups of belonging: individual vs. individual, guild vs. guild, faction vs. faction, server vs. server.

A team hastily thrown together with strangers already lacks any sense of belonging, and if you add in Xfaction, this kind of battle is guaranteed to be a total shitshow.

If the only way to balance the power between two sides is through cross-faction play, then such a battlefield should only exist in server vs server scenarios.

> - Address the "burst-heavy" meta by reducing damage classes deal in PvP. However, I wouldn’t go the route of Resistance, as it separates PvP from PvE and divides the community, which would be a shame. Instead, I recommend using existing game mechanics, particularly Magic Resistance and Armor.

>  - PvP gear needs an update, and simply increasing stats isn't meaningful (as raids will always surpass it, and more raids will be added). I would add increased Armor and significant bonuses to Magic Resistances to each piece of the PvP set (e.g., +40 Fire Resistance on helmets, +40 Frost Resistance on shoulders). This would ensure classes deal less damage to each other. This gear would also be useful for PvE players, as Magic Resistances are essential for certain encounters.
>
>  - Add Holy Resistance among the schools of Magic Resistances.

Resistances should be a bigger thing in wow I completely agree. If holy resistance was a thing one could gear specifically against paladins (which is the most played class on the server). Professions could have more items that boost resistances.

Resistances on more pieces of gear would be a fun way to gear not just in pvp but in PvE as well.

PvP Reward Overhaul & New Gear:
 * New exclusive Arena sets and weapons.

 * Existing rank-based PvP sets and weapons requirements for acquisition will be reduced by 2 ranks:
 -Lieutenant's/Champion's blue boots/gloves will become available starting from Rank 5.
 -Field Marshal’s/Warlord’s epics will become available starting from Rank 10.
 -Weapons will become available from Rank 12.

 * Resilience: All PvP gear (Rank sets, Arena sets) will feature the Resilience stat, reducing damage taken from players.
 * Lieutenant's/Champion's blue(rare) Battleground sets will become as effective in PvP as epic pve sets due to Resilience.
 * Field Marshal’s/Warlord’s (Epic) sets and new Arena sets will surpass all PvE gear in player-versus-player scenarios.

## Post 146891 by Alcilis — 2025-08-19T01:51:00Z
<!-- url: https://forum.turtlecraft.gg/viewtopic.php?p=146891#p146891 | page 11 | era: pre-1.18.1 -->

In my humble PvPer (with 20 years on Vanilla) 's opinion :

About the honor rework :

 - **Leave the formula as it is, without the ranking decay.**
Meaning that at higher ranks you still have to grind enough each week in order to progress, or else your rank progression wont go up, but it can't go down anymore even if you play casually for a while and do only 5k honor for a few weeks at 90% R12/13 for instance.
You wont progress while playing casually, but when you'll be ready to push again you can resume where you were for these last painful % towards next rank.
 - **Rework the R14 requirements a little bit** so it's still a huge grind but doable a bit more casually without having to be the top 0.1/0.2% honor-standing player for weeks... Once you've done it once in your life, you never wanna have to do it again...

About rated arenas :

 - Why not, but **be careful in doing so that it doesn't remove the essence of Vanilla pvp to switch towards TBC pvp**...
To prevent this :
 - Make it so **rated arenas only gives exclusive titles and cosmetics** to distinguish the strongests amongst us, **NOT** stronger gear than the pvp ranking gear (unlike TBC).
To keep the core of Vanilla pvp, **the pvp top-ranks gear should be the best gear for pvp and overall a top-tier gear able to kill even the strongest monsters**.
After all, it's the exclusive gear reserved for the greatest champions of the Horde/Alliance, and as such it should be the best the faction has to offer !
Which leads us to the next point.

About the pvp itemization update :

 - R7-10 gear should be updated to be equivalent to the current R12-13 gear, as it's already quite a grind to get to R10 in a meta where a pve player can already be full T2.5 in half that play time.
 **- Solid weapons, around 80 item level, should be offered at R11 to help pushing towards higher ranks.**
 - R12-13 gear should be updated to end-game level gear.
 **-> Each time a new pve endgame tier-set appears, we should be able to upgrade our pvp gear to a new updated version, either through gold or through BG marks (or adding a new honor-based currency, which would be ideal but maybe more difficult to put into place).**
 - **R14 weapons should at all times be the best weapons in the game in terms of base stats.**
As it was during 95% of Vanilla. They are the most time-consuming items to get in the game, and as such they should be the most rewarding to get.
They should also be updated and upgradeable each time a new pve tier appears.
 - Level 60 pvp reputation rewards should also be reworked in order to provide really solid epic off-sets items instead of shitty 3/4 blue sets as it stands now for BR and AB...
 - **Please change the bonus on the pvp gloves (R7/12) for paladin**, as improved jugement on crusader seal is useless in pvp...
And overall, rework all these specific pvp gear bonuses to **make them specially appealing in terms of pvp QoL for each classes.**

About other pvp changes that should be done :

 - **Balance AV.**
Simply (greatly) reducing the numbers of guards and fixing the archers' LoS in FWK would do much in favour of balancing things a bit.
Horde can jump from the bridge to the first DB tower without aggroing a single npc, while alliance as to aggro 20+ npcs and archers to get to the first FW tower...
 - Increase the amount of carriable BG marks to 100 (because... why not ?)
 - **Either remove Sunny Glade Valley from the daily pvp rotation, or add a specific pvp reputation linked to it, with specific pvp-oriented trinkets as rewards** (inspired by the trinkets found inside the BG, just a thought) to give it some appeal.

Nothing difficult to do, only simple adjustments that would give pvp a second birth.

## Post 154880 by Stmichael — 2025-10-08T18:14:00Z
<!-- url: https://forum.turtlecraft.gg/viewtopic.php?p=154880#p154880 | page 12 | era: 1.18.1-announced-pre-release -->

PvP in WoW has always been a mess. The game fundamentally is designed for PvE. There have been attempts in retail over the years and decades to tweak how PvP works to create a balanced and functional game mode, but with limited success.

Why? Because PvE is designed around an archetype that doesn't exist in PvP: Tanks. Blizzard opted not to give tanking specs the tools to fill a damage mitigating role in PvP, and as a result you either get insane burst damage instagibbing people or PvP gear with resilience.

I think the answer comes from a similar, yet different game: Warhammer Online. Yes, it failed spectacularly largely due to poor map design and technical performance issues, but the core of its combat design philosophy has a lot to learn from. In the case of tanks, they had a number of baseline abilities that helped them stay alive in a large fight, but also extend some of their survivability to their allies. To name a few that were baseline to all tanks:

Guard - toggle ability that would transfer 50% of all incoming damage from the chosen friendly target to the tank (and also reduce their threat generated so this couldn't be abused in PvE to give main tanks a permanent shield wall.)

Taunt/AoE taunt - Targets affected by taunt deal 25% less damage to all targets other than the person who casted the taunt. Lasted 15 seconds or until you attacked the taunter 3 times.

Hold the line (required a shield) - Channeled ability to grant friendlies behind you 15% dodge/resist chance against ranged attacks and spells for the duration. Stacked up to 3 times.

There were others, but this is the direction I would suggest you look in to make pvp more functional. Add in tools for defensive specs that disrupt the instagib builds.

## Post 164726 by Fugus (Patch Note Conspiracy Theorist) — 2026-01-09T05:37:00Z
<!-- url: https://forum.turtlecraft.gg/viewtopic.php?p=164726#p164726 | page 12 | era: 1.18.1-announced-pre-release -->

Arena: I have no problems with people who want to do arena, but don't require arena for the high end PvP gear. I personally never liked it on retail. Even winning, I hated it. BGs, Duels, World PvP and City raids were more my thing. On that note, the old Halaa fights in Nagrand was great in TBC.

You can let arena or rated PvP get the gear a little faster but don't make it required or the alternatives such a slog to get that they aren't worth it anymore. Blizzard killed PvP in WotLK with how they sacrificed all the other PvP trying to force players into the sandbox for duels.

As far as resilience goes, I personally don't care for too much because either it is so low that it gets ignored or so strong that it kills any hopes of set diversity stacking it. Best bet would be just releasing newer PvP gear with the newer content with increased item levels and maybe more varied set bonuses to increase the diversity there as well. Would suck watching all the PvPers of every class all trying to farm the exact same gear setup with exactly no diversity while also running the near exact same talent setup to match the gear they went with. You can have good raid gear be decent at PvP and good PvP gear be decent at raids if that helps allow for diversity of builds and playstyles without actually changing the class/spec identity in the process.

## Post 164731 by Fugus (Patch Note Conspiracy Theorist) — 2026-01-09T07:46:00Z
<!-- url: https://forum.turtlecraft.gg/viewtopic.php?p=164731#p164731 | page 12 | era: 1.18.1-announced-pre-release -->

> **Xudo wrote: Fri Jan 09, 2026 7:37 am**
> But people tend to find most optimal talent setup and optimize gear for it. It is absolutely irrelevant to resilence.

Min/Maxers definitely do. But players who actually want to try and PvP as something other than just the cookie cutter meta might not want to.

Not irrelevant to resilience unless they make as many varied PvP sets and items as they do for PvE which we all know they aren't going to do.

As far as resilence goes, if they went that route they would likely have to implement it so that it reduced as damage and drains from PvP sources by X%.

They couldn't do it on critical strike damage or chance as that would hit crit classes worse than dot based setups which would also require a reduction in the healing and regeneration of healing classes to compensate and all of that will struggle when dealing with the mushroom that is warlocks.

## Post 165295 by Shnitzl — 2026-01-16T00:08:00Z
<!-- url: https://forum.turtlecraft.gg/viewtopic.php?p=165295#p165295 | page 12 | era: 1.18.1-announced-pre-release -->

Please for the love of god, do not introduce Resilience as another band aid fix for the game.

Resilience itself is anathema to the vision of the WoW world, and moves the needle closer to retail than classic. It creates a min max element that further breaks down the world into separate categories. It’s great that you can see people bring their pvp gear to raids and vice versa in bgs. It will make the power disparity worse in gear as those with resil will be able to tank an entire team of newbies without it.
Everyone is just going to end up stacking resil which flattens the skill aspect and makes it more deluded.

I remember healers running around with 400 resil just soaking up hits and keeping entire BGs at bay. Now imagine that healer is a druid in bear form.

Honestly, Just take a look at  the talent trees and abilities that are globaling people. You have classes like Shaman and Rogue that clearly need to be looked at and just retool them and work down from there. Why is it we always have to add something on top of a problem as a fix instead of just looking at the main cause and trying to work backwards from there? Pretty soon all those fixes will add up to some unholy tower of babel like SoD did.

As for addressing the honor grind, can BGs be made cross server? Or just take any one of the dead zones that  no one goes to, like Tel’Abim and turn it into a perpetual AV/Wintergrasp/invasion that people can pop into to complete objectives and pvp for honor. Maybe make it an hourly or daily thing similar to snowball that provides some loot and a small buff.

## Post 165308 by Fugus (Patch Note Conspiracy Theorist) — 2026-01-16T06:37:00Z
<!-- url: https://forum.turtlecraft.gg/viewtopic.php?p=165308#p165308 | page 12 | era: 1.18.1-announced-pre-release -->

What the guy above me said. Resilience was one of those stats were you either had it too ineffective to bother with and is ignored or too effective and was stacked to the hilt. Virtually impossible to balance well across every class and spec.

And if it is effective it ends up killing any gear or spec diversity that strays away from what the PVP sets prioritize.

General rules of thumb:
 1) If the DPS is too strong for PVP, it is too strong for PVE. Tone it down and rebalance PVE accordingly.

 2) If the healing is too strong for PVP, it is too strong for PVE. Tone it down and rebalance PVE accordingly.

 3) If a spell is too powerful for PVP, it is too powerful for PVE. Remove it from the spell book and rebalance PVE accordingly.

The luxury of PVE is you can tweak the health pools, mana pools, mitigation, damage, healing and mechanics to your hearts content without breaking anything outside of that encounter. Not so with abilities, stats, and gear in PVP.

## Post 171718 by daleking0518 — 2026-03-22T03:26:00Z
<!-- url: https://forum.turtlecraft.gg/viewtopic.php?p=171718#p171718 | page 12 | era: post-1.18.1 -->

Please reduce the mana cost of Affliction Warlock spells!

In PvP, the mana consumption is way too high, leaving the spec with almost no room for meaningful gameplay—we run out of mana extremely quickly. Even using mana potions and life tap cannot compensate for this issue. The original mana costs for this spec were perfectly fine; there was no need to change them.

In PvP, you can’t constantly use life tap (trading health for mana) all the time—it only makes you far more vulnerable and increases the risk of being killed.

Please adjust the mana costs for Affliction Warlock, or even revert them back to previous values.

## Post 174838 by Mastermind90 — 2026-04-11T17:14:00Z
<!-- url: https://forum.turtlecraft.gg/viewtopic.php?p=174838#p174838 | page 12 | era: post-1.18.1 -->

Dear developers,

the patches regarding new skills, PvP, damage, and damage reduction are insane and not in a good way, but in a bad way.

Leaving aside the fact that the new abilities could be good to some extent, if they are not balanced, you break the game and this is where the general damage issue comes in.

I can’t say I’ve played all classes, but I have played rogue, druid, and mage.
I’m not here to say what works or doesn’t work in these classes, but I want to understand where you got your calculations and numbers from to change certain parameters.

Concrete example
Frost Mage Frostbolt does 550 to 600 damage with 455 Spell Power, full new PvP set and off set, including enchants for damage, not stamina.
A Frostbolt takes 2.5 seconds to cast and can be interrupted.

Now, can you explain to me why it should deal less damage than Fire Blast
WHY What sense does that make
None.

Rogue
Dissolving poison was nerfed and that’s fine, but can you explain why, in your patches, it is basically impossible to have a dagger like Perdition Blade as BiS
No, you just don’t like a normal rogue.

Before, you pushed everything toward poison and attack speed. Rogues were literally buying daggers, because items like Perdition Blade were considered mediocre in that context, when it should be the exact opposite.

I don’t even want to make a full list, let’s just draw a veil over warriors arms. Brothers, you are in my heart, I really mean it. At this point, just give these warriors the ability to break paladin bubble and mage ice block too, everything is already messed up anyway.

It makes no sense that arms warrior is the worst class right now I’ve ever seen in vanilla. I don’t play warrior, but that’s how it looks.

And regarding hunters, guys, I really hope you enjoy critting 1.6k Arcane Shot and your pets hitting up to 2.1k with Kill Command. Congratulations to the devs who made you like this, I have no words.

That said, after letting off some steam, since people are sharing their suggestions, I also have a list.
I don’t know if it will ever be heard, but I want to try, it can’t get worse than this anyway.

 1 Damage reduction
You can put all the damage reduction you want, but study it properly for each class and talents.
Putting 15 percent or 30 percent for everyone makes no logical sense. Specs like Shadow Priest get completely destroyed, unplayable, just trash.

Balance the damage properly. I’m not a developer, but if you give me the chance, I will take 30 experienced PvP players and we will fix this together, because right now it is unacceptable.

It is not possible to see one class doing extremely high damage and another doing not even half. What kind of balance is that

 2 PvP community feedback
There is a huge effort from PvP players, both on the forum PvP section and on Discord.

Take some time to talk to them. Pick players with many PvP hours, listen to them a bit. Give us a chance to speak.
And even if the answer is a hard no, at least explain your reasoning.

Right now it feels like a dictatorship, and not even a good one in my opinion.

 3 PvP gear
The PvP set is fine for me, but I want to point out something small.

Even if some people say that those who reached rank 11 to 14 cheated with premades or stayed AFK in battlegrounds, they still spent time. They were there, in front of the screen, playing your game.

Rank 14 is prestige, just like T3.5 or higher.
It is fine that new PvP players can access this gear, but speaking for Ambershire, do not hold this gear until Kara 40, release it earlier.

High rank players feel a bit mocked, myself included, even if this is not the highest priority.

For me, you can even do nothing about this last point, but please review your PvP patch.

I don’t have perfect solutions just by writing on a forum, but I am sure that if you ask experienced players like Boombringer, Vegaxs, Goblox, Cornerguy, Eemo, Pir, Guldai, TinyTiny, Buri, Eidson, Redcross, Ctf, Gizzy, Liberty, Xorgan, Jacksparrow, Pinksmycol for example,

the community can at least give you some useful advice.
And if you still don’t want to listen, at least every path has been explored.

Thank you for your attention.

> **Mastermind90 wrote: Sat Apr 11, 2026 5:14 pm**
> Dear developers,
>
>  the patches regarding new skills, PvP, damage, and damage reduction are insane and not in a good way, but in a bad way.
>
>  Leaving aside the fact that the new abilities could be good to some extent, if they are not balanced, you break the game and this is where the general damage issue comes in.
>
>  I can’t say I’ve played all classes, but I have played rogue, druid, and mage.
>  I’m not here to say what works or doesn’t work in these classes, but I want to understand where you got your calculations and numbers from to change certain parameters.
>
>  Concrete example
>  Frost Mage Frostbolt does 550 to 600 damage with 455 Spell Power, full new PvP set and off set, including enchants for damage, not stamina.
>  A Frostbolt takes 2.5 seconds to cast and can be interrupted.
>
>  Now, can you explain to me why it should deal less damage than Fire Blast
>  WHY What sense does that make
>  None.
>
>  Rogue
>  Dissolving poison was nerfed and that’s fine, but can you explain why, in your patches, it is basically impossible to have a dagger like Perdition Blade as BiS
>  No, you just don’t like a normal rogue.
>
>  Before, you pushed everything toward poison and attack speed. Rogues were literally buying daggers, because items like Perdition Blade were considered mediocre in that context, when it should be the exact opposite.
>
>  I don’t even want to make a full list, let’s just draw a veil over warriors arms. Brothers, you are in my heart, I really mean it. At this point, just give these warriors the ability to break paladin bubble and mage ice block too, everything is already messed up anyway.
>  It makes no sense that arms warrior is the worst class right now I’ve ever seen in vanilla. I don’t play warrior, but that’s how it looks.
>  And regarding hunters, guys, I really hope you enjoy critting 1.6k Arcane Shot and your pets hitting up to 2.1k with Kill Command. Congratulations to the devs who made you like this, I have no words.
>  That said, after letting off some steam, since people are sharing their suggestions, I also have a list.
>  I don’t know if it will ever be heard, but I want to try, it can’t get worse than this anyway.
>
>  1 Damage reduction
>  You can put all the damage reduction you want, but study it properly for each class and talents.
>  Putting 15 percent or 30 percent for everyone makes no logical sense. Specs like Shadow Priest get completely destroyed, unplayable, just trash.
>  Balance the damage properly. I’m not a developer, but if you give me the chance, I will take 30 experienced PvP players and we will fix this together, because right now it is unacceptable.
>  It is not possible to see one class doing extremely high damage and another doing not even half. What kind of balance is that
>
>  2 PvP community feedback
>  There is a huge effort from PvP players, both on the forum PvP section and on Discord.
>  Take some time to talk to them. Pick players with many PvP hours, listen to them a bit. Give us a chance to speak.
>  And even if the answer is a hard no, at least explain your reasoning.
>  Right now it feels like a dictatorship, and not even a good one in my opinion.
>
>  3 PvP gear
>  The PvP set is fine for me, but I want to point out something small.
>  Even if some people say that those who reached rank 11 to 14 cheated with premades or stayed AFK in battlegrounds, they still spent time. They were there, in front of the screen, playing your game.
>  Rank 14 is prestige, just like T3.5 or higher.
>  It is fine that new PvP players can access this gear, but speaking for Ambershire, do not hold this gear until Kara 40, release it earlier.
>
>  High rank players feel a bit mocked, myself included, even if this is not the highest priority.
>  For me, you can even do nothing about this last point, but please review your PvP patch.
>  I don’t have perfect solutions just by writing on a forum, but I am sure that if you ask experienced players like Boombringer, Vegaxs, Goblox, Cornerguy, Eemo, Pir, Guldai, TinyTiny, Buri, Eidson, Redcross, Ctf, Gizzy, Liberty, Xorgan, Jacksparrow, Pinksmycol for example,

## Post 174850 by Ragnar2 — 2026-04-11T19:03:00Z
<!-- url: https://forum.turtlecraft.gg/viewtopic.php?p=174850#p174850 | page 12 | era: post-1.18.1 -->

> **Akalix wrote: Sun Mar 30, 2025 7:55 pm**
> **Greetings!**
>
>  Over time as we have gone further down the path of **Vanilla+** and departed from 1:1 vanilla parity, we've made significant changes to all aspects of the game. With this in mind, there are a handful of potential new features and design choices we would like feedback on before implementing. Please note that we reserve the right to make unpopular decisions if we think they will be beneficial to the overall health of the network. As of now, we are not confirming any of these changes, only confirming that we plan to do **something**.
>
>
>  **Honor Rework**
>
>  The current implementation of **Honor** is a slightly more forgiving version of the original vanilla system, where the optimal way to rank up is not improving your gameplay, it's increasing how much time you spend grinding honor. The system, especially at the higher levels, requires an unsustainable amount of queue spamming. There are numerous ways we could change this system—and we would like to know what changes the community would like to see, including if people would rather see no changes at all.
>
>
>  **Rated PvP**
>
>  **Rated PvP** is a commonly requested feature, as the current Honor system doesn't serve as a true skill indicator, rather a indication of time played. At our current population, we **do not** plan to add a rated Battleground feature, as we do not have the PvP population to support it. However, we are considering adding **Rated Arena Teams** and/or a **Solo Shuffle**-esq queue. These appeal to two different types of players, with arena teams being premade and coordinating their play, and a solo shuffle queue perfect for players who would rather log on and "just go" rather than wait for a team.
>
>
>  **PvP Itemization Update**
>
>  Currently because there's limited ways to itemize specifically for PvP, PvP encounters are very bursty, it's kill or be killed.
>
>  This was "solved" after vanilla by introducing full item sets with Resilience on them.
>
>  We would like the community's input on ways we could limit this burst-paced gameplay and have already written out a small list of things we think might work:
> - **PvP Set Bonuses** which would give strong effects for PvP combat.
>
> - **PvP (instanced) Item Buffs** akin to retail's current approach, which buffs PvP items significantly when in PvP.
>
> - **Resilience** or a similar system, which provides stats specifically beneficial to PvP from PvP gear.
>   With this, we would much prefer to change the balance of PvP via itemization, rather than bloating abilities with pvp-exclusive conditionals.
>
>
>  **Closing**
>
>  In closing, I would like to reiterate that none of these changes are directly confirmed—only a handful of concepts that we are open to pursuing if our community is interested in them. Please let us know your feedback on these ideas, as we are actively looking for our community's opinions.

I doubt Turtle will read this but as someone who has already played with all this changes on different servers i think i can tell you in advance how will this changes go.

 1.Honor Rework. The current system is good since it is still Vanila and people who come on Turtle want to experience Vanila. The only prob is the decay. Let people grind when they have time since most people here have jobs and cant play all day long just to counter the decay. Without decay the longer people play PVP on Turtle the more rewarded they will be. (This is basicly a copy from what Blizard did and was seen as positive change)
 2.Rated PVP. Thats ok and it is what most other private servers have. However other servers have not changed the classes like Turtle, so there is far more balance in PVP. Here with two Rouges or two Warlocks you would be unbeatable in arena. So i doubt many people will play it even tho people think it is a good idea since they haven't actually tried it. I say allow Rated PVP but leave BGs as the main option to take the best PVP gear like it is on Classic WOW or your current system. If you go completely arena as on TBC,LK servers you will compete with them for PVP community and since they are way more balanced i think Turtle would loose for sure. Your main appeal for PVP community is actually your Classic BG based system.
 3.PvP Itemization. Im against resilience since similar as in number 2 it is moving more towards TBC server rather then Classic. This would also be a big hit for the PVE community since their gear won't be good for PVP, and one of the main things here for many players is to get good PVE gear and than use it in PVP. I think both gears should be about the same strength. As for the burst problem, some classes are bursty but some are not at all. Usually burst classes lack stuns and cc spells so you can kite them but if they reach you, you pay for that. I think the new gear ads a lot of HP which should solve the burst prob, and in most cases players who suffer from burst are just low geared or even low levels fighting twinks. Its kind of normal when someone has way better gear for that to make a big difference. If you want to remove burst in PVP completely, then you will need to rework the burst classes to have stuns/cc. Cause its a two way street, just see how you damped dmg and that did made burst classes weaker, however it also made healers and kiting/cc classes stronger, some even op.
